# eye_v2 E1 report

E1 is complete within the declared map-and-front-end scope. G1-G5 pass
software consistency tests; biological gates unassessed. No network
simulation, neuron drive, spike conversion, or biological fitting occurred.

## Coverage and provenance

All 45,528 assignment rows match graph root IDs;
0 assignment rows are outside the graph. There are
31 matched assignment types and 1,581 mapped column keys
(785 left, 796 right). Graph type and side disagreements are both zero.
Of 138,639 graph cells, 93,111 lack assignments across ALL types.
Among the 31 assignment-covered graph types, 47,484 cells occur in the graph;
1,956 of these lack assignments. These two missing-cell totals have different denominators.

Source copies are labeled FlyWire FAFB v783. Their SHA-256 values, including
compressed bytes, identify the exact inputs; release authenticity is not
independently certified. coordinates contains root/position/supervoxel fields,
visual_neuron_types contains categorical annotations, consolidated_cell_types
contains type annotations. Neither these schemas nor the supplied design note
provides a hex-to-angle transform. Soma positions are not used as optical axes.

The graph table below uses graph type and hemisphere as denominator. Assigned
means an exact root-ID match; ranges are over matched cells in that group.
The separate assignment table uses source-file labels and includes outside-graph counts.

## Gates

| Gate | Verdict | Numerical evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| G1 | passed | All shared-prefix magnitudes and polarities bit-equal |
| G2 | passed | Both modes: graded/signed max error 0; signs and start samples exact |
| G3 | passed | 1,581 targets; 2,499,561 comparisons; false positive/negative 0/0; tolerance 4 degrees |
| G4 | passed, convention only | 785 left and 796 right responses; cross-side increases 0/0 |
| G5 | passed negative control | Corrupted G3 fails: 121,908 false positives and 121,908 false negatives |
| Biological | unassessed | No visual recording targets in supplied evidence |

The permutation preserves every target's affected-column count and total first-sample response
to maximum sum error 5.6843418860808015e-14 (threshold 1e-12).
Its oracle uses the original frozen column identities, not the corrupted coordinates.
This establishes that the G3 test detects broken assignments. It does not establish
anatomical orientation, optical accuracy, biological selectivity, or photoreceptor identity.

## Declared constants and limitations

Every entry below has no biological source:

| Constant | Value | Role |
| --- | --- | --- |
| EYE_HEX_STEP_DEG / Config.hex_step_deg | 2 degrees | Declared interommatidial step |
| sample_rate_hz | 1000 samples/s | Computational sampling, not output rate |
| tau_lowpass_ms | 5 ms | Causal first-order smoothing |
| tau_sub_ms | 50 ms | Inferred subtractive adaptation |
| tau_div_ms | 100 ms | Inferred mean-square feedback |
| strength | 1 | Arbitrary graded-unit gain |
| divisive offset | 1 | Arbitrary graded-unit denominator |
| background, contrast scale | 0.5, 0.5 | Rectangle luminance convention |
| initial states | 0 | Trial initial condition |
| horizontal origin margin | 0.5 hex step | Keeps hemispheres on opposite sides of zero |
| laterality sign | left +1, right -1 | Declared, unverified hemisphere convention |
| axial embedding | u=p+q/2, v=sqrt(3)*q/2 | Mathematical hex convention |

The per-hemisphere origins are derived once from the full assignment table:
`{"left": {"u0": -23.5, "v0": 0.0}, "right": {"u0": -23.0, "v0": 0.43301270189221963}}`.
This is a declared convention, uncalibrated; the step remains configurable.
No alternative coordinate convention was selected after observing gates.
Outer pools R1-6 as a channel name, with R7 and R8 separate only by name.
All three use identical temporal filters: no measured class differences are supplied.
Channels are not cell assignments; missing R1-6 hexes are not fabricated.
Output is one column-by-sample magnitude and polarity array per class, never Hz.
`eye_absolute_rate_calibrated=false`; `eye_rate_ceiling_hz=null`.

Sequential rank is not a coordinate. The existing eye.py orders each L1/L2
family by body ID, spreads ranks over azimuth and holds elevation at zero.
The new join instead retains published p,q assignments by root ID, with
unmatched cells explicitly counted. Even those assignments do not supply
the optical-angle or hemisphere transform; the declared convention remains
separate from the publication's integer column evidence.

## Scope note on the eye

> The visual front end in flybench/eye.py places L1/L2 cells by sorted body
> ID at zero elevation and drives both families with the same positive rate.
> The supplied design note identifies a conflict with inhibitory L1 signs
> in the graph. That front end is a synthetic delivery test and does not
> establish spatial or physiological visual transduction. Historical visual
> results remain stress tests of that delivery scheme. The preregistered
> eye2 replacement provides graded signals over joined column assignments
> with declared angle conventions; it has no network-drive interface.

This adapts the supplied draft to the completed E1 scope, removes unsupported
rate claims, and attributes the L1 sign observation to the supplied note.

## Verification, scope and deviations

Final targeted pytest: 54 passed, exit 0.
Intentional harness probe: 1 failed, exit 1.
Both count lines and exit codes were inspected. Tests use synthetic inputs;
real-map metrics are recorded separately, not inferred from pytest status.
Tests cover exact large-integer IDs, absent/duplicate/conflicting data, origin
stability, explicit modes, invalid-input nonmutation, scalar recurrence,
both temporal polarities, class equality, reset, empty/irregular chunks,
finite-height rectangles and a deliberately wrong assignment oracle.
Only the two authorized test modules were run; no full suite or simulation.
The prescribed Windows Python/dependency chain was used with locale C,
bytecode disabled and pytest plugin autoload/cache disabled. No environment difference.
Evidence driver wall time: 25.520 seconds.
Reproduce from repository root using the prescribed environment:

```text
<python> build/records-raw/eye_v2_e1/run.py
<python> build/records-raw/eye_v2_e1/finalize.py
```

No protected source tree or archive was opened. Only dependency imports used
the explicitly authorized external dependency directory. No git writes occurred.
Legacy eye.py, world, README and all pre-existing tracked files are unchanged.
The five summary files are below 5 MB; the audit records byte counts, hashes,
BOM checks and git status. All delivered text is English UTF-8 without BOM.
The preregistration hash was recorded before implementation; source hashes
were recorded before gate evaluation and rechecked at finalization.
No gate, seed or threshold changes. Operational inventory caveat: the visual
publication has intrinsic/boundary categories, not a definitive columnar flag.
Thus absent intrinsic/retinal candidates and naming candidates are explicitly
reported as candidates; they must not be read as confirmed columnar anatomy.
No held-out anatomical prediction was performed: the task specifies spatial
self-consistency and permutation control in place of the draft note's proposal.
The requested metadata identifiers and phrase 'declared convention, uncalibrated'
are retained as explicit task exceptions to the prohibited affirmative vocabulary.

Next step: network delivery is a separate round requiring Akif's seal.

## Frozen preregistration (verbatim)

```text
# eye_v2 E1 preregistration

Frozen before implementation and gate evaluation on 2026-09-17. Budget: 60
minutes from first inspection. No network simulation, neuron drive, fitting,
git writes, or edits to the legacy eye or world. SHA-256 is recorded before
implementation. Only the supplied FAFB v783 copies and design note are used.

## Mapping and coverage

Join column_assignment.root_id to graph.body_id as exact integers. Reject
duplicate root IDs and conflicting (hemisphere, column_id) hex coordinates.
Retain unmatched counts and label disagreements; never invent missing hexes.
Column keys include hemisphere. Use the full assignment file to freeze the
coordinate origin; expose only columns with at least one graph-matched cell.
Coverage uses graph type and side as denominator, counts assigned by exact ID,
and separately records assignment-file type/hemisphere coverage and mismatches.
Report all graph types, and flag absent assignment types among visual intrinsic
and retinal categories from visual_neuron_types as an operational columnar
candidate inventory, not a complete anatomical classification. Also report
every graph type beginning R, L, Mi, Tm, T4, T5, C or T followed by digits as
a naming-based candidate; distinguish these two inventory sources explicitly.
No inference from soma coordinates, connectivity, or ID order.

No hex-to-angle transform is supplied in the inspected data schemas or note.
EYE_HEX_STEP_DEG=2.0 is a design constant: no biological source.
For axial p,q, u=p+q/2 and v=sqrt(3)*q/2. Within each hemisphere of the FULL
assignment table, u0=min(u)-0.5 and v0=(min(v)+max(v))/2. Angles in degrees:
azimuth=s*(u-u0)*step; elevation=(v-v0)*step. s=+1 for left hemisphere and
-1 for right. Negative azimuth means left visual field. Thus left field maps
to right hemisphere by declared, unverified convention. This is a declared
convention, uncalibrated. No source-based optical axis, field boundary,
interommatidial angle, or contralateral biological mapping is asserted.

## Contract and equations

Explicit modes: column_luminance, a finite time-by-column matrix in [0,1],
and object_parametric, a time-by-5 matrix of bearing_deg, elevation_deg,
width_deg, height_deg, contrast. Width/height are positive full extents;
contrast is in [-1,1]. One axis-aligned rectangle per sample, no angular
wrapping. Luminance is background 0.5 outside, 0.5+0.5*contrast inside,
including edges. This rasterization is an explicit part of object mode,
not an implicit conversion between input modes. Instances reject mode changes.

Output has one (column, sample) graded magnitude array and polarity array per
named class outer (R1-6 pooled), R7, R8. All three use identical filters:
no measured class differences are available. These are abstract channels,
not assignments of R1-6 cells or evidence that each column has all classes.
eye_absolute_rate_calibrated=false; eye_rate_ceiling_hz=null. No spike API.
Sampling rate 1000 samples/s, low-pass tau=5 ms, subtractive adaptation tau=50
ms, divisive adaptation tau=100 ms, strength=1; all have no biological source.
First-order low-pass f=a*f_prev+(1-a)*x, b=a_sub*b_prev+(1-a_sub)*f,
u=f-b, d=a_div*d_prev+(1-a_div)*f*f; signed output u/(1+strength*d).
Each a=exp(-1000/(sample_rate_hz*tau_ms)). Magnitude=abs(signed), polarity=sign(u).
All states initially zero, continuous across calls, reset only explicitly.
Empty chunks do not advance state; invalid input is rejected before mutation.
Window length only controls I/O. No biological parameter fitting.

## Frozen gates and negative controls

G1: seed 2801, 200 samples uniform [0,1] over all mapped columns; two signals
share 100 samples and have independently drawn suffixes. Every class's graded
and polarity prefix must be bit-equal, including processing the prefix alone.
Lookahead or trial normalization would violate this gate.

G2: seed 2802, 200 samples, whole versus chunks with boundaries
[0,1,1,7,23,24,80,131,199,200]. Maximum graded and signed error <1e-9;
polarity equal and start_sample correct. State reset per chunk would fail.
Both modes are tested, with object trajectories over mapped column centers.

G3: target each mapped column's azimuth in turn, full width=4*step (tolerance
2*step), elevation=0, height=2*(max(abs(elevation))+step), contrast=1.
From a fresh zero state, exactly the columns with |azimuth-theta|<=2*step
must have greater first-sample graded response than the background-only
trial; all other columns must equal background bit-for-bit. Require zero
false positives and zero false negatives across all targets. This is spatial
self-consistency, not map accuracy. Also test finite-height rectangles on
synthetic coordinates to detect discarded elevation or width/height swaps.

G4: every mapped left-hemisphere azimuth >0 and right-hemisphere azimuth <0;
objects centered at signed midrange with width equal to that hemisphere's
azimuth span plus one step must increase only that hemisphere's response.
Require nonempty responses on both sides and zero cross-side increases.
Biological laterality remains declared, unverified.

G5: seed 2805, uniformly permute coordinate pairs across fixed column keys,
including hemispheres; project the same G3 targets through the corrupted
map and score against the ORIGINAL frozen key-to-angle table. At least one
G3 false positive and one false negative must occur. Per-target affected
column counts and summed first-sample responses must remain identical to
the intact map (sum tolerance 1e-12). This catches a vacuous spatial oracle.

Biological gates unassessed: the supplied evidence has no visual recording
targets. No biological pass is inferred from G1-G5. Synthetic tests also use
independent recurrence and exact integer join oracles, invalid/duplicate data,
reset, empty calls, and nonmutation after rejected inputs. A separate intentional
pytest failure must show both one failure and exit 1 before acceptance.

## Evidence and reporting

Freeze preregistration, all four compressed source files and graph hashes.
Use prescribed Python/dependency chain, locale C, no bytecode, no plugin
autoload, pytest -p no:cacheprovider. No environment fallback. Store scripts,
raw mapping, gate details and logs only under build/records-raw/eye_v2_e1/.
Five summary records total <=5 MB; English UTF-8 without BOM, no machine paths.
Report verbatim preregistration, full coverage table, G1-G5, biological status,
all design constants and source limitations, scope note, test counts/exits,
hashes, git status, and deviations. An audit omits its own recursive hash and
records that exclusion. No external literature claims or downloads are added.
Next step: network delivery is a separate round requiring Akif's seal.
```
